<a id="becoming生成之论的形式核心--the-formal-core-of-the-theory-of-becoming"></a>
# Becoming: the formal core of the Theory of Becoming

One relational unfolding, stated once. This directory is the Lean package `Becoming`. The canonical chapter gives the account; the unification plan gives the direction and phases.

<a id="三环--three-rings"></a>
## Three rings

| Ring | Contents | Constraint |
|---|---|---|
| Core | The root `Becoming` and the three layers inside the package, L0 to L2, resting on the three foundation modules `GenerativeCore`, `InquirySufficiency` and `RelationalRenewal`, which are checked with the core and not counted in it ([08 §3](../../../../../../theories/becoming/chapters/08-METHOD.md#rings)) | Imports only the foundation and itself, its root `Becoming` aside, which also imports the instance and application ring; every source declaration, the foundation's included, has an empty axiom report (for constructors with fields Lean generates injectivity lemmas `injEq` that depend on `propext`; no source declaration uses them, and the checker lists them one by one); compiles from Lean's built-in prelude alone, with the foundation's `import Std` removed |
| Instances and applications | Instances read general laws of the core on one named carrier: the Changes (`Becoming.Hexagrams`, `HexagramPlaces`, `HexagramCommuting`, `HexagramLaws`, `HexagramSites`, `HexagramSequences`), `Becoming.PlaceTransitionNets` and the rooms tested by a window (`Becoming.RoomNet`, `RoomRuns`), where a reader is rebuilt from (`Becoming.ReaderRebuilding`) and where a rule of entry is taught from (`Becoming.RuleRebuilding`). Applications are modules no instance imports and no core module but the root `Becoming` imports: `Becoming.Dispatch`, `GuardHorizon`, `ResponseLearning`, `Coverage`, `SeedReach`, `ScopedReach`, `OpenedCorrection` (`INSTANCES`, `APPLICATIONS` in the checker, R35b) | The core's conditions (imports only the core and the ring, empty axiom reports, compiles from the prelude); outside the core by direction: no core module but the root `Becoming` imports a ring module, and no instance imports an application, which the checker asserts |
| (Bridge) | None since the F3 sweep (19 October 2026): the bridge modules and the [periphery joins](#periphery-joins) are in [`../history/becoming/`](../history/becoming/) with their bytes | The core states the content of the periphery modules they read; those modules are superseded (`SUPERSEDED` in the checker), out of the run |
| Periphery | The existing ethics and dynamics modules, Mathlib leaves and their bridges [`CorrectionInquiry`](../universal-ethics/generative-capacity/CorrectionInquiry.lean), [`LearningCorrespondence`](../universal-ethics/evolution-connectionism/LearningCorrespondence.lean), Python experiments | May use any suitable tool; the direction of dependence points to the core |

Core purity is [Ren's requirement](../../../../../../sources/15-codex-theory-conversations/2026-10-09-pure-core.md). The checker enforces all three conditions on every run.

<a id="四层--four-layers"></a>
## Three layers on one base

`import Becoming` brings in every layer. The core has one base, the run, in L0: what the unfolding is at each step and how it goes on (`Run`, `Becoming.Frame`, on the foundation's `Generative.Process`). The world family (`World`, `Web`, `Flow`) is the standing-and-exchange reading of a run, by definition: a `World` is one kind of relatum and its history, a run of the process that admits every step (`everyStep`), whose configurations are its webs at each step (`WebAt`) and whose moves are its relata's exchanges; `World.web`, `World.flow` and `World.move` read that history along the step index, and `Web F C` is `Dependence Nat F C`, so a law of dependences stated once serves webs. So `Becoming.World` imports the frame, which rests on the grammar (`Becoming.Grammar`, `Becoming.GrammarMap`, `Becoming.GrammarLaws`), `Becoming.Prelude` and the foundation's `GenerativeCore`. A world is also a run of the law it shows, with its history's configurations and moves (`World.run`, `World.run_history`, `World.run_world`, `Run.world_webAt`, `Run.world_move`, all `rfl`, in [`Becoming.Runs`](Becoming/Runs.lean)); a run of a process whose configurations are not webs is read as a world only through its claims, keeping neither its moves nor any standing (`Frame.world`, in `Becoming.Labelled`). A determined law's notions are those of its process by definition: `Law.Lossless`, `Law.Merges`, `Law.Returns`, `Law.Reachable` and `Law.KeepsPossibilities` are abbreviations at `Law.process next`, read on the orbit by `Law.lossless_iff` and its siblings (`Becoming.Recurrence`). `Becoming.Labelled` states the readings of the labelled process from the world's side; where a module states laws of both kinds, its readings are in `Becoming.Labelled`, `Becoming.LabelledRoles`, `Becoming.ValuationFrames` or the run and trace module that reads it (board item W, second batch), a placement of readings, no longer a rule about imports.

| Layer or ring | Module | What it states |
|---|---|---|
| Foundation | [`GenerativeCore`](../GenerativeCore.lean), [`InquirySufficiency`](../inquiry-root/sufficiency/InquirySufficiency.lean), [`RelationalRenewal`](../deeper/RelationalRenewal.lean) | in no layer: checked with the core and not counted in it; processes, traces, composition and transport, on which `Becoming.Frame` and `InquirySufficiency` rest (`GenerativeCore`); bounded sufficient inquiry through actual changing configurations (`InquirySufficiency`); renewing and consuming as a relation to participated conditions (`RelationalRenewal`) |
| L0 | [`Becoming.Grammar`](Becoming/Grammar.lean) | the grammar: a relational calculus for every carrier and relation, names in Chinese with a binding table; continuation (`«续»`) is 缘 met with a class, the positions are read from it and none stands by itself (`«四相无自立»`); a class constant under 缘 never lets a continuation end (`«有常则续不尽»`), so a line's own regularity is its class's core, the greatest symmetric constant relation inside it (`«根»`); for a class, constancy is exactly closure of the coarse net (`«闭合当且仅当有常»`); every occurrence beginning or having a predecessor, in every model, is exactly excluded middle (`«四相之定当且仅当排中»`) |
| L0 | [`Becoming.GrammarMap`](Becoming/GrammarMap.lean) | the map at two grains and on branches: carrying renewal is alteration (`«传则异»`); going on along some branch and ending on every branch exclude each other (`«延尽不并»`); generation, keeping and degeneration on branches, the mixed region inhabited (`Map.«生守相杂»`) and excluded on a single line (`«单行则生守不并»`); renewal does not decide the region; telling going on from ending at every point is exactly excluded middle (`«延尽之定当且仅当排中»`); ending on every branch reaches an end up to double negation (`«尽则非无终»`) and where going on is decided (`«尽而续可定则有终»`), and with no decision exactly under excluded middle (`«尽则有终当且仅当排中»`, witnessed by `Map.StepWhere`, the short life gated by a proposition); along the successor a line reaches exactly the later numbers (`Map.step_reached_le`, `Map.step_reaches_of_le`) |
| L0 | [`Becoming.Prelude`](Becoming/Prelude.lean) | the lemmas on numbers, truth values, lists and options that the layers use, each stated once (no premise; it imports only Init): cancellation, subtraction and products of whole numbers (`add_right_cancel`, `Naturals.sub_sub`, `mul_assoc_nat`), what `all`, `any`, `contains`, `filter`, `find?`, `bind` and `map` test (`ListTests.all_true_iff`, `ListTests.find_none_iff`), and membership in joined and mapped lists (`mem_append_cases`, `preimage_of_mem_map`), proved by recursion where the prelude uses `propext`; the one balance over a stretch (`balance_over_stretch_on`), whose cases are the stretch balances of flows, amounts, courses and censuses |
| L0 | [`Becoming.GrammarLaws`](Becoming/GrammarLaws.lean) | the five shared laws on the grammar, for every carrier and relation (no premise): closure is being the occurrence itself or reached (`«所闭当且仅当同一或及»`), a region every step keeps holding from where it holds on (`kept_onward`), a closed region not gone out of (`«不出»`, `«闭则不出»`), an occurrence's closure among them, the closure law read through `«不出»` (`«所闭不出»`), the closure under the converse being the closure read backward (`«所闭之逆»`), a closure through borrowed steps lying within `o`'s or `a`'s closure, and the two within `o`'s under a larger relation where `a` lies within it (`«借步之所闭在二所闭»`, `«二所闭在借步之所闭»`); perfect pairings of lists (`«对»`) conserve count, are equal count under the one (`«一之对»`), compose, join, gather over a family and carry over a stretch (`«对之接»`, `«对之族»`, `«对之程»`), so the count law is exactly a pairing of units (`«守数当且仅当可对»`, `«程守数当且仅当可对»`), near-definitional; counting a region (`«计»`), a pairing keeps the count on every region not gone out of either way, which is one the relation does not cross and one closed both ways (`«对则不越域守数»`, `GrammarLaws.uncrossed_iff_notGoingOut`, `GrammarLaws.uncrossed_iff_closed`) and, under a class decided by a test, is exactly equal count in every class (`«类之对当且仅当类类等数»`), and the census of a step pairs the units before with those arising to the units after with those ceasing (`«定后定前则前并生对后并亡»`); drawn in is reaching what does not reach back (`«入当且仅当有不返»`), and with the successor fixed at what it reaches an occurrence reaching itself is not drawn (`«定后于则返者不入»`); a listed line merging nothing returns to its start (`«线有限不并则返»`); occurrences ending on lines from a list, the successor fixed where the lines could part, number at most the list (`«线终不过所列»`), and at stages a list covering one stage bounds the endings over a stretch with nothing beginning, mirrored for beginnings (`«间无生则间亡不过所覆»`, `«间无亡则间生不过所覆»`), so with lists at arbitrarily late stages endings recurring need beginnings recurring (`«有覆而亡屡则生屡»`, `«有界覆而生屡则亡屡»` and their undecided forms), the census of a run and the models' covering laws their cases (`«定后前者在前则亡屡者生屡»`); falls whose courses end do not recur (`«尽则不或屡»`), rest no case of being drawn (`«止而不入之例»`, `«入而屡之例»`); within `n` steps composing (`«迭内之接»`), and a course each of whose rounds holds what every condition held holding at round `n` where everything reaching in `n` steps held at the start, with memory within `n` (`«承之迭»`, `«存承之迭»`); steps splitting as they compose and an unending line having steps of every length (`«迭之分»`, `«不穷则迭»`); passing through a word's relations in turn (`«历»`), `«迭»` its case for one relation repeated (`«迭即历»`), six laws of `«迭»` the cases of its laws (`«历之接»`, `«历之逆»`, `«同行之历»` and the others); going alike and constancy (`«局定后则有常当且仅当同行可续»`), a map's image with a fixed successor exactly where sameness under it goes alike (`«像定后当且仅当同行»`), a map preserving a relation exactly where its image lies within the other (`«保之像»`); with a fixed successor a class's core going alike, and with a successor everywhere too the coarsest class inside the class that does (`«定后则类之核同行»`, `«定后遍后则核为其内最粗同行之类»`), off a fixed successor not always (`«后不定则核可不同行»`); widening a strict order of relations (`«广不可逆»`, `«广之传递»`), and a transitive relation joined to the strict part of a transitive relation containing it transitive (`strictJoin_trans`). Frame imports it; the models' laws of closure, finite runs, lumping and order are proved from it, and balance is represented in Flow, not derived |
| L0 | [`Becoming.Frame`](Becoming/Frame.lean) | a frame is a process with realized claims; runs, the one closure of a relation (`WithinClosure`, the grammar's `«所闭»`), reach as the closure of the steps, invariants as regions closed under them, novel realization |

| L1 | [`Becoming.GrammarCandidates`](Becoming/GrammarCandidates.lean) | the corpus's candidate theorems of the grammar tested: correlatives of one relation had together (`«能所相待»`); the way back from a complement exactly double-negation stability (`«相待之还当且仅当双非可还»`); position depends on the class exactly where an occurrence is not its own condition (`«定性之判»`); conditions precede results exactly without cycles (`«缘先果后当且仅当无环»`) |

with the unit fixed, taking from a closed family does not recur (Flow.taking_not_recurring); kept as research notes beside this line: under loss in proportion (Flow.DissipativeBy q: every closed family gets back at most q/(q+1) of what is taken from it, which holds where each formation does so, Flow.DissipatesBy.of_each) the amount law holds at every resolution: everything taken from a family closed from T on, each step's amount counted in the unit of step T (refinedSum), is at most q + 1 times its stock at T, and everything given back at most q times (Flow.uptake_bound, Flow.replenishment_bound), and in a world with a census, embodied relata and every step from T on enclosed, likewise what is taken from and given to what stands (World.taken_bound, World.given_bound); no smaller bound holds: a formation taking a closed pool in full at each step and giving back q/(q+1) of it comes within 1/m of the bound for every m (Geometric, amount_sharp); every count of a size follows from it (counts_of_amounts): the formations taking at least 1/p of the unit from a family closed from T on number in all at most p(q+1) times its stock (Flow.exchange_bound), and in a world with a census, embodied relata and every step from T on enclosed, the relata gaining at least 1/p of the unit number in all at most p q times the total standing (World.growth_bound), so such gains, and arising at that size, do not recur (World.growth_not_recurring, World.arising_not_recurring); the relata falling by at least 1/p of the unit number in all at most p(q+1) times the total standing (World.fall_bound), so neither arising nor ceasing at that size recurs (World.not_recurring_at_resolution, rest at each size, at every resolution); where each active step beyond support lowers the reserve by at least 1/p of the unit (Web.CarriesAt), an unsupported formation is active at most p times its reserve (Web.CarriesAt.lasts), the spine's reserve being the case of p one at a fixed unit (Web.Carries.carriesAt, Web.carriesIn_one); at a unit splitting without end each boundary is witnessed: without a least size a closed pool is taken from without end (Asymptote) and a new relatum arises at every step inside an enclosure (Emission, giving back half of what is taken), at sizes shrinking without bound, neither balanced at the fixed unit; without loss in proportion a whole unit is exchanged at every step (Circulation), or arises in turn on two sides inside an enclosure (Alternation), losses shrinking without bound, neither dissipative in any proportion; without a least fall of the reserve a formation never supported is active at every step, its reserve falling at each (Dwindle); a least loss at each size (Flow.LosesAtSize) gives the exchange law (Flow.exchange_bound_of_least_loss) but not the growth law: a whole unit passing in ever more, ever smaller transfers arises without end and is taken without bound in amount (Trickle, growth_needs_proportion, throughput_needs_proportion) |

so read, the universal reason follows from the condition with no seed, neutrality or connection premise (`universal_reason_of_unrestricted`); C read on possibilities, what the levels of a course admit: under the condition extension under this reading is retaining and adding what has significance (`Ranking.possible_extends_iff`); as the whole-run reading does, it ranks the absence of an overdrawing formation above its occurrence, restraint above overdraw, also with a third formation, and sharing above winning by consumption, and it withholds no event (`Ranking.possible_ranks`, `Ranking.possible_restraint`, `Ranking.ThirdParty.possible_extends`, `Ranking.possible_contest`, `Ranking.malignant_possible`); a formation drawing within renewal adds no possibility, its presence and absence extending neither the other, while both readings of `living` rank its presence higher (`Ranking.possible_tie`, `Ranking.readings_rank_presence`, `Ranking.sharing_presence`); a level path nowhere lower is never ranked below (`Web.not_added_of_le`); the readings are the AI's: positive relevance is read from significance, and courses are compared on possibilities |

The layers are computed (`development/r31-support/layers.py` in the R31 kit, which checks each row's label and can write this table): L0 is the base, `Becoming.World` with every module of the package it imports (`Becoming.Frame`, `Becoming.Prelude` and the grammar's `Becoming.Grammar`, `Becoming.GrammarMap` and `Becoming.GrammarLaws`, on which the frame rests), on the foundation, whose three modules are in no layer and head the table; L2 holds the modules whose source states the continuation condition or a valuation (`Unrestricted`, `Valuation`) and every module importing one of them; L1 holds the rest. No module imports one of a higher layer, which the script checks. The instance and application ring is outside the core by direction and in no layer; its rows are labelled Instance or Application. Within a layer the rows follow an import order.

<a id="periphery-joins"></a>
## Periphery joins

U2 (17 October 2026) stated the laws of the periphery modules in the checker's run on the base, each join reading a periphery structure as a process, run, trace, frame or web; joins through periphery proofs with non-empty axiom reports were named `…Inherited`. The port (F3, 18–19 October 2026) stated that content in the core, and the sweep moved every join, with the bridge modules, to [`../history/becoming/Becoming/`](../history/becoming/Becoming/) with its bytes ([port program](../../../../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/development/bridge-fold/PORT-PROGRAM.md)). The checker still checks joins (`PERIPHERY_JOINS`), were one added.

<a id="检查--verification"></a>
## Verification

```bash
python3 -B experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/check_becoming.py
```

The checker resolves the import closure, requires inherited modules to match passed receipts, compiles in dependency order with Lean 4.29.1, audits the axioms of every source declaration and of every constant Lean holds for the core closure, and checks the core's three purity conditions. The receipt records the result; each run's folder in `becoming-checks/` keeps the receipt, the drivers and logs of the steps that ran, and copies of the source and checker files `main` does not hold byte for byte (from 12 October 2026; the rest are named by the receipt's `source_commit` and sha256).

Each Lean step is keyed by everything it reads: the toolchain and binary, its own bytes and, through their keys, the bytes of its whole import closure. A step whose key matches one that passed earlier is not run again; its outputs and log come from a cache shared by runs and worktrees (`~/.cache/becoming-lean`, or `BECOMING_LEAN_CACHE`), so only changed modules and the modules depending on them are compiled and audited again. The receipt's `lean_steps` lists the steps run and the steps reused, each with the run it came from. `BECOMING_FRESH=1` runs every step; `LEAN_TIMEOUT` sets the seconds allowed for each Lean run. [`check_correction_inquiry.py`](../universal-ethics/generative-capacity/check_correction_inquiry.py) uses the same cache, with the Mathlib revision in its keys.

To build changed or new modules in scratch, use [`scratch_build.py`](scratch_build.py) `TREE [MODULE ...]`: TREE holds a session's changed or added modules at their package paths and every other module comes from this checkout. It compiles the import closure with the checker's command, toolchain and step keys, reuses passed steps from the cache and stores the steps that pass, so the landing run and other sessions reuse them; it then audits the named modules' declarations with the checker's own `#print axioms` driver, cached the same way. It is not a receipt: the environment audit, the controls and the prelude-only compile of the core run only in the checker.

<a id="自行验证--verify-it-yourself"></a>
## Verify it yourself

1. Install elan, the Lean toolchain manager: `curl https://raw.githubusercontent.com/leanprover/elan/master/elan-init.sh -sSf | sh`, then the package's toolchain: `elan toolchain install leanprover/lean4:v4.29.1` (the one this folder's [`lean-toolchain`](lean-toolchain) names). Python 3 (standard library only) is also needed; with git, the receipt names the files `main` holds by its commit, and without it every file is copied into the run folder.
2. From the repository root, with no cache and the output outside the repository:

```bash
BECOMING_FRESH=1 BECOMING_OUT=/tmp/becoming-run \
  python3 -B experiments/integrated-correction/general-framework/generative-basis/lean/becoming/check_becoming.py
```

3. `"status": "passed"` in the last line of JSON means all of the following were checked on your machine. **Core purity**: the core's import closure is exactly the foundation, the core modules and the instance and application ring, which no core module but the root `Becoming` imports, all files of this repository (no Mathlib, no `Lean` metaprogramming). **Empty axiom reports**: `#print axioms` reports no axiom for every source declaration of the foundation and the core. **Prelude-only compile**: with `import Std` removed, the core closure compiles from Lean's prelude alone, warnings failing the run. **Environment audit**: every constant Lean holds for the core closure and for the bridge modules (generated constants, private declarations and unnamed instances included) depends on no axiom, the one exception being constructors' injectivity lemmas (`injEq`) on `propext`, none of them a source declaration. Also: the Lean version must be 4.29.1 and the binary's sha256 is recorded; the control examples compile.
4. Environment variables: `BECOMING_FRESH=1` runs every step without the cache (the cache is outside the repository and unauthenticated, so use it for any claim made to others); `BECOMING_OUT=<dir>` writes the run folder and the receipt to that directory and leaves the repository unchanged; `BECOMING_JOBS` sets how many Lean steps run at once (default half the cores, 1 for one at a time); `LEAN_TIMEOUT` sets the seconds allowed for each Lean run (default 600; raise it on a loaded machine); `BECOMING_LEAN_CACHE` sets the cache directory (default `~/.cache/becoming-lean`); `LEAN` names the Lean binary, else what `elan which lean` finds in this folder is used, else elan's default path.

The periphery checkers that use Mathlib (such as `check_correction_inquiry.py`) depend on a Mathlib project outside the repository: `SYSTEMS_MATHLIB_PROJECT`, by default `~/repos/生生不息`, with toolchain v4.30.0-rc2, not the core's v4.29.1. Its `lakefile.lean` and `lake-manifest.json` are kept byte for byte in [`../periphery-mathlib-pin/`](../periphery-mathlib-pin/README.md), pinning the Mathlib revision; the core check above does not need it.

<a id="扩展--extending"></a>
## Extending

- State new formal results here; extend the core rather than build another model.
- Existing modules keep their scope; where their content belongs to the core, it is stated in the core and the module is registered as superseded (the F3 port), rather than connected through a bridge.
- Results needing Mathlib or other libraries live in the periphery and import the core, never the reverse. `CorrectionInquiry` is one, checked with Lean 4.30.0-rc2 and Mathlib; the core compiles there too.

<a id="名称--names"></a>
## Names

Modules are named for what they state. Declaration names, all in namespace `Becoming`, did not change when the modules were renamed, so record aliases remained valid.

| Former module | Module | Why |
|---|---|---|
| `BecomingFrame` | `Becoming.Frame` | package structure |
| `BecomingModal` | `Becoming.Inquiry` | it states the scope of inquiry, not modal logic |
| `BecomingRenewal` | `Becoming.Renewal` | package structure |
| `MetaethicalCore` | `Becoming.Metaethics` | the core is the root and all three layers, not this layer alone |
| `BecomingInstances` | `Becoming.Bridge` | it bridges core and periphery |

The foundation keeps its historical namespace `Generative`. Its processes are any becoming, including maintenance and degeneration, not only generative change.

<a id="声明改名--declaration-renames"></a>
### Declaration renames

These declarations were renamed after the fourth independent review (H1, H6, H13) and Ren's correction of foundational vocabulary (“付费记录”太市场；“recipient, money, pay” 不是最好的选词; gloss: "paid record" is too market-like, and "recipient, money, pay" is not the best choice of words); the affected records' aliases were updated with them.

| Former | Now | Why |
|---|---|---|
| `Web.MetSince` | `Web.MetAbove` | the minimum form: below a need that was met and below the present need |
| `Web.Paid`, `paid_finitely` | `Web.Depletes`, `depletion_not_recurring` | depletion of conditions, not payment |
| `conserved_ceases` | `dissipation_arising_not_recurring` | the premise that matters is dissipation; stated for any quantity that never rises |
| `Flow.load`, `Flow.supply` | `Flow.uptake`, `Flow.replenishment` | uptake and replenishment |
| `Flow.ledger`, `Flow.accumulates` | `Flow.Balances.over`, `Flow.Balances.accumulates` | a balance, not a ledger |
| `takers_le_load`, `supply_le`, `More.supply_unbounded` | `takers_le_uptake`, `replenishment_le`, `More.replenishment_unbounded` | as above |

Later renames, among them those of 14 October 2026 (A1–A11, B1–B5 and N of the general-laws plan, e.g. `MakesSense` → `IdentifiesAlike`, `Denial` → `Negation`) are tabulated in [plan §10](../../../../../../_process/research/2026-10-05-generative-foundation-review/history/root-foundation/BECOMING-UNIFICATION-PLAN.md#10-第一部分与第二部分--part-i-and-part-ii).
